**Daily live-acquisition fail-fast and zero-recovery corrective V1 (2026-09-03):** `DAILY_LIVE_ACQUISITION_FAIL_FAST_AND_ZERO_RECOVERY_CORRECTIVE_V1 = COMPLETE` at `9baf3483a594415d2ecba5d230ef146006b8677f` (base `a0fe2b21a2969a47669a3438045484fd84a56a50`). Owner-directed bounded corrective fix, not a roadmap feature milestone (not registered in `docs/ROADMAP_STATE.json`). Live defect: the owner ran `.\stocklookup.ps1 daily` on 2026-09-03 at ~18:33 Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh, after `DAILY_OWNER_FLOW_POST_CLOSE_STABILIZATION_GATE_V1`'s new 15:30 floor. P3F9B correctly targeted `2026-09-03` and returned exact-session coverage 17/1683, but the pipeline continued through 17 liquidity batches and technical-history recovery before `tools/run_market_wide_current_technical_coverage_scaleout.py --consolidate` crashed with `FileNotFoundError` (its `out` directory, previously created only as a side effect of `run_batch()`'s own `mkdir(parents=True)`, never existed because zero recovery candidates meant `run_batch()` never ran). The `CalledProcessError` this raised was caught by `canonical_daily_operation.run_canonical_daily_operation()`'s generic `except Exception` around the acquisition call and relabelled `STAGE_BLOCKED_ACQUISITION`, so the owner terminal incorrectly printed `Status: POST_CLOSE_DATA_NOT_READY` for a genuine code defect.

**Three coordinated fixes, all found and scoped from the real incident.** (1) `consolidate()` now creates its output directory (`output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)`) before writing, so a legitimate zero-candidate recovery cohort consolidates to the real empty artifact `build_recovery_artifact()` already produces instead of crashing; ordinary non-zero consolidation and a genuinely missing (non-zero-candidate) batch are unaffected. (2) `canonical_post_close_pipeline.acquire_and_materialize()` previously ran the entire `daily_session_level2_package.materialize_independent_components()` (liquidity batches, technical-history recovery, and every other downstream step) before ever checking `MIN_EXACT_SESSION_COVERAGE_RATIO` -- so a thin/partial snapshot paid for the full downstream chain before being rejected, exactly what happened live. The P3F9B acquisition step is extracted into a new, reusable `daily_session_level2_package.ensure_exact_session_snapshot()` helper (byte-identical acquisition logic, just a clean boundary); `acquire_and_materialize()` now acquires the snapshot, validates coverage, and only then invokes the rest of materialization -- a session below the 0.20 floor stops immediately, before any liquidity batch, technical-history recovery, or triage build runs. `materialize_independent_components()` calls the same helper first for backward compatibility, so every existing standalone caller is unaffected. (3) `canonical_daily_operation.run_canonical_daily_operation()`'s acquisition-stage exception handling previously collapsed a deliberate `CanonicalPostCloseError` (thin coverage, session mismatch, ...) and any unexpected exception (`FileNotFoundError`, `CalledProcessError`, a programming defect) into the same `STAGE_BLOCKED_ACQUISITION` "not ready" stage. A new `STAGE_FAILED_ACQUISITION_PIPELINE` stage -- deliberately excluded from `PRE_ACQUISITION_NOT_READY_STAGES`/`POST_ACQUISITION_NOT_READY_STAGES`/`NOT_READY_STAGES` -- now carries any exception type other than `CanonicalPostCloseError`, so it prints as a genuine failure (`format_owner_daily_status`'s existing generic-stage branch) and exits `1`, never `2`, and is never mistaken for routine data lag.

**Not changed:** the 15:30 post-close attempt floor, `MIN_EXACT_SESSION_COVERAGE_RATIO = 0.20`, DNSE acquisition/authority semantics, `working_dates` semantics, Financial V2, valuation, technical formulas, Integrated Decision policy, Dashboard, or `ai-core-private`. `resolve_acquisition_root`'s existing retained-partial-snapshot redirect (a same-session artifact below the coverage floor is byte-preserved and never reused; a fresh, distinctly-named attempt root is selected instead) was already correct and is unchanged -- confirmed by a new dedicated test using the live incident's own 17/1683 shape, not just the pre-cutoff-timing case the prior milestone tested. Today's real `operations-review/p3f9b-market-wide-exact-session-scaleout-20260903/` (17/1683) and `operations-review/market-wide-current-liquidity-research-v1-20260903/` evidence in the primary checkout were not read, edited, or deleted by this fix; a subsequent owner rerun decides reuse/redirect under the existing (unchanged) eligibility contract.

**Verification.** 24 new/updated focused tests across 5 files (`test_run_market_wide_current_technical_coverage_scaleout.py` new, 5 tests; `test_daily_session_level2_package.py` +4; `test_canonical_post_close_pipeline.py` 6 updated + 1 new; `test_canonical_daily_operation.py` +2 parametrized; `test_daily_analysis_pipeline.py` +1 new, 1 strengthened), covering: zero-candidate consolidation (directory creation, semantic emptiness, no fabricated records, ordinary/missing-batch regressions); low coverage (the real 17/1683 shape) fails immediately with liquidity/technical-recovery/triage asserted never-called, while sufficient coverage still proceeds to the normal downstream path; the retained-partial-snapshot-on-rerun redirect for a below-floor coverage artifact; and exception-type classification at both `canonical_daily_operation.run_canonical_daily_operation()` (`CanonicalPostCloseError` to `STAGE_BLOCKED_ACQUISITION`/not-ready vs. `FileNotFoundError`/`CalledProcessError`/`TypeError` to `STAGE_FAILED_ACQUISITION_PIPELINE`/failure) and `daily_analysis_pipeline.main()`'s owner-status/exit-code wiring. Full bounded affected-domain regression (`test_daily_session_level2_package.py`, `test_canonical_post_close_pipeline.py`, `test_canonical_daily_operation.py`, `test_daily_analysis_pipeline.py`, `test_stocklookup_daily_owner_status.py`, `test_completed_market_session_gate.py`, `test_market_wide_current_technical_coverage_scaleout.py`, `test_next_session_decision_brief_posture_transition.py`, `test_dashboard_domain_freshness.py`, `test_canonical_dashboard_runtime_release.py`): exact failure-ID match (10 failures, identical set) against a controlled `git stash` baseline in the same fresh worktree -- zero new failures. All 10 are confirmed pre-existing: 9 are gitignored, worktree-local `operations-review/`/registry evidence absent from a fresh worktree (independently confirmed passing on the primary checkout); the 10th, `test_canonical_post_close_pipeline.py::test_level2_p3f9b_acquisition_uses_real_cli_flags`, is the stale test this milestone's own brief explicitly named and instructed not to act on (`tools/run_p3f9b_market_wide_exact_session_scaleout.py` does define and correctly consume `--session`; re-confirmed against current code). `py_compile`, `git diff --check`, and `tools/stocklookup_roadmap.py --check` (`ON_TRACK`, `PASS`) all clean. No production Daily run was executed by the fixing session; the owner reruns `stocklookup.ps1 daily` manually. **Daily owner-flow post-close stabilization gate V1 (2026-09-03):** `DAILY_OWNER_FLOW_POST_CLOSE_STABILIZATION_GATE_V1 = COMPLETE`. Owner-directed bounded corrective fix, not a roadmap feature milestone (not registered in `docs/ROADMAP_STATE.json`). Live defect: on 2026-09-03 after market close, `.\stocklookup.ps1 daily` returned `DAILY_OPERATION_STATE=TOO_EARLY` / `BEFORE_SAFETY_FLOOR,NO_DEFENSIBLE_INTENDED_SESSION` because `completed_market_session_gate.DEFAULT_SAFETY_FLOOR` was `time(18, 0)`, an unnecessarily conservative floor given full-universe (HOSE/HNX/UPCoM) market activity ends by ~15:00. Owner decision: the post-close acquisition attempt floor moves to **15:30 Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh** (30 minutes after the 15:00 market-wide activity boundary, for provider stabilization) -- still an operational trigger window, never market-session factual authority or provider-confirmed completion; `provider_confirmed_completed` stays `false` throughout.

`completed_market_session_gate.py` gains `DEFAULT_POST_CLOSE_ATTEMPT_FLOOR = time(15, 30)` as the honestly-named canonical constant; `DEFAULT_SAFETY_FLOOR` is kept as a compatibility alias (same object) so every existing importer and the serialized `safety_floor` record field keep working unchanged. No Phase A/B logic changed -- `evaluate_attempt_eligibility`/`evaluate_completed_market_session_gate`/`_acquired_after_safety_floor`/`_exact_session_sufficient` already took `safety_floor` as a parameter, so the value change alone moves the real behavior. A second, independently-hardcoded 18:00 was found and fixed in `canonical_post_close_pipeline.POST_CLOSE_COLLECTION_CUTOFF_LOCAL_TIME` (used by `assert_same_day_post_close_eligible`/`assert_post_close_eligible`, both directly in `canonical_daily_operation.run_canonical_daily_operation`'s call chain) -- left unfixed, the owner would still have been blocked until 18:00 despite Phase A reporting `ATTEMPT_ELIGIBLE` at 15:30. It is now single-sourced from `completed_market_session_gate.DEFAULT_POST_CLOSE_ATTEMPT_FLOOR` rather than its own literal, so the two gates cannot drift apart again. `tools/run_capability_first_eod_operation.py` (the separate `CAPABILITY_FIRST_EOD_1800_OPERATIONAL_SCHEDULING_V1` product) and `bitemporal_semantic_contract.EOD_RESEARCH_CUTOFF` were deliberately left at 18:00: the former reads its own persisted `config/capability_first_eod_operation.json` (`safety_floor_local_time: "18:00"`), not this Python default, and is a genuinely separate, differently-scoped tool; the latter is unreachable from the daily owner flow (consumed only by `temporal_retention.py`/`asof_query_ledger.py`). Neither is "a second competing floor" for the path this milestone fixes.

Owner CLI UX: `canonical_daily_operation.py` gains `format_owner_daily_status()` plus `PRE_ACQUISITION_NOT_READY_STAGES`/`POST_ACQUISITION_NOT_READY_STAGES`/`NOT_READY_STAGES`, so a pre-floor run prints a clean `STOCK LOOKUP DAILY` / `Status: CURRENT_SESSION_STABILIZING` block (current time, session candidate, the actual evaluated floor, latest governed completed session if known, `Publication: NOT ATTEMPTED`) and a post-floor-but-insufficient-evidence run prints `Status: POST_CLOSE_DATA_NOT_READY` (session, `Publication: BLOCKED`, exact reason codes, `Action: rerun later`) -- both distinct from a genuine pipeline defect, which still names its real stage. `daily_analysis_pipeline.py --canonical-post-close` prints this block and now also exits `2` (not-ready, not `1`) for `BLOCKED_ACQUISITION`/`BLOCKED_POST_ACQUISITION_SESSION_MISMATCH`, matching the existing `TOO_EARLY`/`NON_WORKING_DATE`/`FUTURE_SESSION`/`PROVIDER_EVIDENCE_UNAVAILABLE`/`BLOCKED_PRE_ACQUISITION_SESSION_EVIDENCE` convention. `stocklookup.py`'s `daily` command no longer prints a redundant, generic `STATUS: FAILED_PRODUCER` over an exit code `2` the subprocess already explained cleanly (new `_producer_failure_message()`); a genuine failure (any other nonzero code) still gets it. No sleep/poll/retry/scheduler was added anywhere in this diff.

Required boundary matrix verified with injected `Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh` timestamps (never the host wall clock): 14:59/15:00/15:15/15:29:59 stay `TOO_EARLY`; 15:30:00/15:35/17:00 are `ATTEMPT_ELIGIBLE` for the current qualified working date (17:00 specifically guards against ever regressing to the old 18:00 rule); omitted- and explicit-`--session` both resolve the current session at 15:30; exact-session evidence at 15:30+ satisfies the gate's time component while 15:20 does not; 15:30 with no or thin (10/1683) exact evidence stays fail-closed; session/exact-evidence mismatch, future session, and weekend/holiday non-working dates are unaffected and still fail closed. 40 tests in `tests/test_completed_market_session_gate.py` (was ~33; 8 new functions, one a 7-case parametrized sweep), plus new/updated coverage in `tests/test_canonical_post_close_pipeline.py`, `tests/test_daily_analysis_pipeline.py`, and a new `tests/test_stocklookup_daily_owner_status.py` (18 new test functions total across the diff) -- 134 passed in the combined focused run across all five touched modules' test files. No financial/valuation/technical engine, integrated decision policy, prospective outcome logic, market-dashboard, or ai-core-private file changed. **Canonical daily Financial V2 and current research enrichment V1 (2026-09-03):** `CANONICAL_DAILY_FINANCIAL_V2_AND_CURRENT_RESEARCH_ENRICHMENT_V1 = COMPLETE / COHERENT_PARTIAL_BY_RETAINED_EVIDENCE` at `f3880c589d345ff20e67fd2df84acdaf57f4681e` (base `cfe05ef17e91d7a3863ca7bebe5a8ee89820f43b`, == `daily_integrated_decision_brief`'s own feature commit `90e3260a5596fa02c210fcc13bb3d1b28d8b329f`'s docs closeout). Closes the exact gap that milestone's Phase 0 found and deliberately deferred (see `docs/DECISIONS.md` 2026-09-02 decision 5): Financial V2 had no canonical daily-materialization path anywhere in `canonical_post_close_pipeline.py`, so `_integrated_investment_decision_product()`'s `financial_analysis_artifact` fed the legacy, undated `market_wide_current_fundamental_research/v1` artifact (or, in a fresh worktree lacking that specific force-tracked file, nothing at all) instead of the flat `financial_analysis_product_integration/v1` compact shape `evaluate_fundamental_direction()` actually reads -- silently degrading `fundamental_state` to `INSUFFICIENT` for every ticker, every session. This session's own empirical audit (task section 10, "verify empirically, do not assume the wiring is correct") found a **second, parallel real defect** in the same closure: `current_valuation_artifact` fed the raw, unevaluated `market_wide_current_valuation_input_scaleout` artifact (price/EPS/BVPS-shaped) instead of the evaluated `current_research_valuation_context/v1` shape (`methods`, `peer_relative_context`) `evaluate_valuation_context()` needs -- confirmed by cross-checking `tools/run_integrated_investment_decision_replay.py`'s own real `load_inputs()`, the one place both correct shapes were already proven end to end.

**What shipped.** Two new modules. `financial_v2_current_input_authority.py`: a small, pinned, versioned pointer to the retained Financial V2 input chain (`market-wide-structured-financial-period-semantics-v1-20260831/`, `market-wide-fundamental-feature-store-v1-20260831/`, `market-wide-financial-entity-classification-scaleout-v1-20260901/`) -- force-tracked directories present identically in every checkout -- with pinned expected artifact identities verified at load time (`FINANCIAL_V2_INPUT_AUTHORITY_IDENTITY_DRIFT` fails closed on silent evidence drift); never a `.glob`/mtime "latest folder" scan (source-pattern-tested). `canonical_daily_financial_v2_materialization.py`: `build_engine_artifact()` (the raw `financial_analysis_context/v2` engine, via the unmodified `market_wide_financial_analysis_v2_scaleout.build_scaleout()`, with the current tracked `entity_classification_contract` layered over the frozen classification-scaleout diagnostics snapshot -- the same fix `CORE_FUNDAMENTAL_VALUATION_AND_PEER_CONTEXT_V1` established); `build_compact_product()` (the `financial_analysis_product_integration/v1` compact product via the unmodified `financial_analysis_product_projection.build_product_projection()`, projected over the **Daily Product's own ticker denominator**, not the engine's narrower cohort -- every ticker gets an explicit `AVAILABLE` or `ABSENT` record, zero silent drops); `build_peer_context()` (sector/industry peer context via the unmodified `current_research_valuation_context.attach_engine_fundamental_peers()`); `build_evaluated_valuation_artifact()` (the raw valuation artifact evaluated via the unmodified `evaluate_ticker_valuation()`/`attach_peer_relative()`, joined against the same engine artifact's TTM features); and `build_session_artifact()`, the full canonical session-delivery wrapper binding `decision_session` to the financial evidence legitimately used, carrying `financial_evidence_as_of_period` and `financial_evidence_period_range` distinct from `decision_session` itself. No formula is duplicated anywhere -- every builder is orchestration glue over already-governed engines.

`canonical_post_close_pipeline.py`'s `_integrated_investment_decision_product()` closure now builds the Financial V2 engine once and the compact product + evaluated valuation from it fresh each session (mirroring the closure's own existing pattern for the V3 tactical projection and relative volume), over the same daily denominator (`sorted(p3f9b["records"].keys())`) already used for relative volume, and persists both at new canonical per-session paths (`daily_session_level2_package.session_artifact_paths()`'s new `financial_analysis_product`/`current_valuation_evaluated` keys: `operations-review/financial-analysis-product-v2-<session>/`). `integrated_investment_decision_product.build_artifact()` gained a fail-closed contract-shape assertion: a **present-but-wrong** `financial_analysis_artifact.contract_version` now raises `INCOMPATIBLE_FINANCIAL_ANALYSIS_CONTRACT` instead of silently degrading (an **absent** `contract_version` stays permitted, so every existing lightweight test/replay fixture that omits it keeps working unmodified) -- this is the literal "cannot recur silently" guard task section 14 asked for, and it explicitly rejects both the legacy contract string and the raw engine contract string, not just one. `daily_integrated_decision_brief.py` gained an additive `financial_evidence_context` section and `build_from_session()` now loads and threads the new canonical artifact through, so a downstream AI reader can state "fundamental evidence is based on FY/Q reporting period X, while market/technical evidence is session Y" without guessing. `export_ai_bundle.py`'s `attach_financial_analysis_v2_product_context()` now auto-resolves the canonical same-session artifact exactly like `attach_integrated_investment_decision_product()` already does (its pre-existing opt-in contract -- `include=False` ignores `artifact_path` entirely, no I/O -- is preserved byte-for-byte; only the previously-always-required-explicit-path behavior gained an auto-resolve fallback), so a normal `stocklookup.ps1 daily` run needs no extra financial flags to get `tickers[ticker].financial_analysis` in the AI bundle once the canonical artifact exists.

Delivers the final daily research product: new `daily_integrated_decision_brief/v1` (`daily_integrated_decision_brief.py`) -- a pure join over `integrated_investment_decision_product/v1`, `next_session_decision_brief/v2`, `current_market_sector_leadership_context/v1`, and `current_opportunity_prioritization/v1` producing `market_summary`, `sector_summary`, deterministic `opportunity_sets` (7 sets: `ACTIONABLE_NOW`/`RETEST_CANDIDATES`/`EARLY_SETUPS`/`EXTENDED_DO_NOT_CHASE`/`HOLD_MANAGE`/`RISK_AVOID`/`INSUFFICIENT_RESEARCH`, documented ordering method, top-10 shortlists, no universal score), the canonical 11-ticker `watchlist` (via `owner_research_focus.broader_watchlist()`), `decision_transitions`, a descriptive `what_changed_today` projection, `risk_summary`, and `feedback_status`. New `integrated_decision_prospective_feedback.py` bridges every session's already-durable integrated-decision records (session-scoped, immutable, content-addressed by construction -- no separate admission/store step needed for retention itself) to a forward-outcome walk over the session's own retained multi-session P3F9B price history, reusing `classify_feedback_taxonomy()` directly via a minimal shim (same engine, same labels, not a second backtest engine); close-path fields are explicitly named `max_favorable_close_excursion`/`max_adverse_close_excursion` (never MFE/MAE) with an explicit `CLOSE_ONLY_PATH_STATISTIC_NOT_TRUE_INTRADAY_MFE_MAE` semantics flag. Wired end to end into `stocklookup.py`'s real `daily` command (automatic, best-effort, non-blocking, no extra owner flags -- if the current session's integrated-decision artifact is genuinely missing it reports and skips rather than silently substituting a stale prior-session product) and into `ai_handoff_publication.py` (new optional, additive `daily_integrated_decision_brief` parameter with full manifest lineage, alongside the already-present `integrated_investment_decision_product_identity`).

**Phase 0 found and fixed two real, pre-existing production defects** in the immediately preceding integration (`5227207`), both confirmed against real 2026-08-25/2026-08-28 evidence and neither present in that commit's own diff: (1) `integrated_investment_decision_product.py` read `market_regime`/`sector_leadership` from a flat key shape (`breadth_regime`/`sector_relative_context`) belonging to the unrelated `opportunity_context.py:_market_axis()` output, not the real `current_market_sector_leadership_context/v1` artifact `canonical_post_close_pipeline.py` actually supplies (real shape: `market.current_breadth_state`, per-ticker `ticker_contexts[t].sector_leadership_context.leadership_state`) -- these fields were silently constant defaults (`NEUTRAL_MIXED`/`IN_LINE`) in production; the three existing unit tests covering this path had (accidentally) only ever validated the wrong shape. Context that is legitimately session-independent (fundamental cross-sectional scoring, valuation-research proxy, corporate events, TTM, A1/A2 temporal) is reused exactly as the canonical Daily Producer pipeline already treats its own analogous `fundamental`/`catalyst` dimensions — not recomputed, not treated as stale. `daily_research_session_operations.build_operation()`/`run_session_operation()` gained one new optional `shadow_security_recommendation` parameter threaded to the pre-existing `current_daily_decision_research_product.build()` hook; omitting it reproduces prior behavior byte-for-byte, so no canonical run can newly fail globally from this capability. The bounded 2026-08-27/2026-08-28 replay produced real same-session artifacts (523-name governed denominator, zero residual, both days) and the Session Bundle now retains them verbatim for 75/106 (27th) and 83/123 (28th) tickers; the remainder is explicit `TICKER_NOT_IN_UPSTREAM_SHADOW_SECURITY_RECOMMENDATION` — outside the governed fundamental cross-sectional cohort, a pre-existing upstream coverage boundary, not a defect. Recommendation/invalidation label distributions differ session to session (e.g. `HIGH_RISK_SPECULATION_ONLY` 15→22), proving genuine same-session re-evaluation rather than relabeled stale data. The 27→28 lifecycle replay's 58 comparable records are unchanged; of those, 41 now carry retained recommendation+invalidation context (was 0), all 41 `UNCHANGED` label-to-label with zero newly-activated/cleared invalidations over the one trading day — a valid, evidenced zero-transition result, not a gap — while the pre-existing UIC/VTV material tactical-confirmation-loss transitions reproduce unchanged. `authority_effect = NONE`; no recommendation or invalidation rule, threshold, or label vocabulary was changed anywhere in this milestone. Genuine remaining boundary: roughly 30% of Session Bundle tickers sit outside the 523-name governed fundamental cross-sectional research cohort established by the earlier `FUNDAMENTAL_CROSS_SECTIONAL_SCORING_AND_RANKING_V1` milestone; widening that cohort is a distinct, separate milestone, not something this orchestration should or does override. Artifact: `operations-review/daily-session-shadow-recommendation-v1-20260830/validation_artifact.json`.

**A3 as-of query and revision ledger V1 (2026-08-29):** `A3_ASOF_QUERY_AND_REVISION_LEDGER_V1 = OUTCOME_A3_ASOF_QUERY_AND_REVISION_LEDGER_ACTIVE_NO_AUTHORITY_PROMOTED`. New `asof_query_ledger/v1` contract provides a deterministic, fail-closed knowledge-cutoff query and revision ledger over already-retained A1/A2 temporal evidence. `query_as_of(observations, as_of_research_session)` returns one of five explicit result codes — `READY` (exactly one qualifying observation), `UNKNOWN_TEMPORAL` (insufficient evidence, fail closed), `NOT_YET_KNOWABLE` (observation exists but knowledge boundary is after cutoff), `NO_QUALIFYING_OBSERVATION` (no evidence supplied), or `CONFLICT` (multiple irresolvable knowable candidates) — with a deterministic revision ledger and per-entry `knowability_at_cutoff` flags. `build_revision_ledger` exposes the full ordered revision sequence. PIT safety invariants: (1) no observation whose `knowledge_available_research_session` is after the as-of cutoff is ever selected; (2) `KNOWLEDGE_UNKNOWN` always fails closed without backfill; (3) `historical_reconstruction_scope == NONE` blocks eligibility; (4) HTTP `Date`/`Last-Modified`/`ETag` are never used for knowledge determination; (5) research-session eligibility does not imply same-session close execution eligibility; (6) ledger ordering is deterministic (knowledge session asc, then observation identity); (7) identical inputs produce byte-identical results. This is infrastructure/semantic capability only: `RAW_AS_TRADED=NOT_PROMOTED`, historical price PIT `BLOCKED`, historical full-system backtest `BLOCKED`, and same-session close execution `NOT_ESTABLISHED` are invariants on every result code. 25 new tests (`tests/test_asof_query_ledger.py`) all pass; A1 9/9 and A2 8/8 regression tests pass with zero regressions. Artifact: `operations-review/a3-asof-query-and-revision-ledger-v1-20260829/artifact.json`. Next temporal capability only if authorized.

**Financial operational proxy foundation and research tier activation V1 (2026-08-28):** `FINANCIAL_OPERATIONAL_PROXY_FOUNDATION_AND_RESEARCH_TIER_ACTIVATION_V1 = OUTCOME_A_OPERATIONAL_FINANCIAL_PROXY_RESEARCH_TIER_ACTIVE`. New `financial_operational_proxy.py` pivots financial-data semantics from binary `NOT AUTHORITATIVE -> NOT USABLE` to fitness-for-use by evidence tier: three explicit tiers (`OPERATIONAL_PROXY`, `VERIFIED_RESEARCH_EVIDENCE`, `AUTHORITATIVE_EVIDENCE`) plus a six-boolean purpose-specific fitness matrix (`display_eligible`/`research_eligible`/`trend_eligible`/`valuation_research_eligible`/`authoritative_financial_eligible`/`pit_backtest_eligible` -- never one collapsed `usable` flag) over already-retained provider-tier canonical financial facts (`canonical_financial_facts.py`/`canonical_fact_store.py`), reconciled against the unmodified P3-F13 official panel via a new six-state classifier (`EXACT_MATCH`/`VALUE_CONFLICT`/`NOT_COMPARABLE_SCOPE`/`NOT_COMPARABLE_PERIOD`/`NOT_COMPARABLE_UNIT`/`NO_OFFICIAL_COMPARATOR`) that reuses the pre-existing, previously-dormant `provider_financial_semantic_basis.classify_provider_exact_research_usable` per-fact citation-agreement route as one of its two upgrade paths -- `EXACT_MATCH` upgrades `OPERATIONAL_PROXY` only to `VERIFIED_RESEARCH_EVIDENCE`, never to `AUTHORITATIVE_EVIDENCE`, and `VALUE_CONFLICT` fails the conflicting observation closed without ever overwriting the official fact. Official-qualified issuer count is unchanged at 13 before and after attach. None of the 6 is merged into the panel (0/6 eligible for ingress; every key collides with an existing entry), so P3-F13 official-qualified cells remain 70 and valuation `RESEARCH_USABLE` counts are unchanged (market_cap 888, P/E 9, P/B 10, P/S 9, EV 9, EV/Sales 9, EV/EBITDA 0; READY/VALUE 0/1,507) — provably a no-op since `merge_document_qualified_facts_into_panel` was never invoked with an eligible key. Bank/securities (VCB/SSI) never reach this module — the existing `entity_type` gate in `extract_candidates` precedes dispatch — confirmed zero corporate facts emitted for either. No network, no OCR, no production DB mutation, no VALUE/ranking/recommendation/PIT promotion. 35 new tests (`tests/test_official_financial_structural_table.py`); 88 total across the six directly-related suites pass; `py_compile`/`git diff --check` clean. **Annual provider financial metadata retention and flow semantic corroboration V1 (2026-08-27):** `ANNUAL_PROVIDER_FINANCIAL_METADATA_RETENTION_AND_FLOW_SEMANTIC_CORROBORATION_V1 = OUTCOME_B_PROVIDER_ANNUAL_METADATA_RECOVERED_SEMANTICS_PARTIAL`. The KBS annual route is now traced to `GET https://kbbuddywts.kbsec.com.vn/iis-server/investment/stock/finance-info/{ticker}`: income uses `type=KQKD&page=1&pageSize=4&unit=1000&termtype=1&languageid=1`; cash uses `type=LCTT&page=1&pageSize=4&unit=1000&termtype=1&termType=1&code={ticker}`. A bounded four-issuer flow cohort (FPT/HPG/NVL/PAN) retained 16 exact raw responses: eight initial contract-mismatch empty JSON bodies (the wrong `periodType` spelling, preserved as evidence) and eight corrected successful bodies, within the 16-call ceiling. The corrected bodies expose and now retain 32 period-level `Head` sidecars: annual start/end, report/update dates, audit status, and KBS `United` label `Hợp nhất` (provider scope = consolidated). The installed adapter had previously discarded these by converting `Head`/`Audit`/`Unit` into transient dataframe attributes; its `unit=1000` request and `×1000` value transform remain explicit but still do **not** establish currency or numeric unit/scale. Exact raw→adapter→observation lineage joins 874/874 annual observations; no production DB changed. The sidecar does not invent source-absent currency/unit/scale. Flow identity reconciliation remains unavailable for promotion (0 agree/transform/conflict, 16 missing-official, 24 missing-provider; residual 0); `provider_financial_semantic_basis/v1` stays at zero absolute-qualified shapes. PVD/VNM untouched. The 12,914-cell inventory and isolated valuation stay unchanged: provider exact 0; RU market cap 888, P/E 9, P/B 10, P/S 9, EV 9, EV/Sales 9, EV/EBITDA 0; READY/VALUE 0/1,507. Artifacts: `operations-review/annual-provider-financial-metadata-retention-and-flow-semantic-corroboration-v1-20260827/`. `authority_effect = NONE`. Next gate: provider-owned currency and numeric unit/scale metadata on the exact annual KBS flow route, plus the same-year official anchors for the exact canonical identities.

**Provider financial semantic basis qualification V1 (2026-08-27):** `PROVIDER_FINANCIAL_SEMANTIC_BASIS_QUALIFICATION_V1 = OUTCOME_C_RETAINED_PROVIDER_ABSOLUTE_SEMANTICS_REMAIN_UNQUALIFIED`. Follow-on inside the existing Layer-3 canonical-financial-facts lane (`canonical_financial_facts.py`/`canonical_financial_resolvers.py`/`canonical_fact_store.py`) and the current-fundamental-research inventory (`financial_fact_coverage_recovery.py`), not a new model, engine, or provider. New `provider_financial_semantic_basis.py` requires BOTH provider-owned schema/library-contract evidence AND zero-disagreement, discriminating (≥2 issuers, ≥5x magnitude), official-anchor reconciliation before any `(provider, statement_family)` shape may qualify — either alone is explicitly insufficient. Installed `vnstock==4.0.4` source code (strictly better evidence than a live probe, since it shows the exact transform applied) proves KBS's adapter requests `unit=1000` and multiplies every value by 1000.0 before returning it (`vnstock/explorer/kbs/financial.py:566,367,259`; 99.97% of a 5,943-row retained sample are exact multiples of 1000), while VCI's adapter has zero unit/scale/currency handling anywhere. Real reconciliation against the 8 tickers carrying an official citation (`dashboard-runtime`, read-only, zero network): `(VCI, balance_sheet)` is the only shape with any evidence at all — 6/8 issuers agree exactly on `shareholders_equity` (8.86T–114.6T VND) and 5/7 agree on `cash_and_cash_equivalents` after fixing a real citation-mapping bug (`canonical_fact_store.load_official_citations` mapped `cash_and_equivalents`→`cash_and_equivalents` instead of →`cash_and_cash_equivalents`, so the citation never matched any built fact) — but PVD (~25,250x) and VNM (~2.7%) reproducibly disagree on both metrics, failing the zero-disagreement bar despite passing the discriminating-anchor one. Every other shape has zero reachable reconciliation: `data_bctc/*_year.parquet` do not exist for any ticker (a pre-existing, never-backfilled filename-collision bug predating `bctc_sync.py`'s 2026-07-12 "P0-1" fix), so no annual provider payload exists to compare against the only retained (annual) citations, and the existing stock-only annual→Q4 alias correctly never extends to flow metrics. Zero shapes reach `PROVIDER_ABSOLUTE_RESEARCH_QUALIFIED`. A narrower, per-fact `PROVIDER_EXACT_RESEARCH_USABLE` tier (zero generalization; requires the fact's own `qualified`/`official_citation_agreement` status plus independently-resolved `statement_scope`) is wired into `financial_fact_coverage_recovery.classify_identity_cell` and proven correct by direct tests, but empirically finds 10 qualifying facts (FPT/HPG/NVL/PAN/POW × 2 identities) that are all for tickers already `OFFICIAL_QUALIFIED` for the same identity — the mechanism is real and requires no further code change, but has zero current beneficiaries because every ticker with an official citation is already in the official panel. Full-universe recompute of the 12,914-cell inventory: **0 cells changed** (`OFFICIAL_QUALIFIED` 64, `PROVIDER_DESCRIPTIVE_ONLY` 2,679, `UNIT_OR_SCALE_UNRESOLVED` 7,817, `MISSING` 2,349, `ENTITY_IDENTITY_MISMATCH` 5, residual 0, before and after identical). Rerun current-valuation artifact is byte-identical to the pre-milestone one (`valuation_unchanged=true`); `metric_research_usable_counts` unchanged (market_cap 888, P/E 9, P/B 10, P/S 9, EV 9, EV/Sales 9, EV/EBITDA 0); `READY`/`VALUE` stay 0/1,507. Also flagged, not fixed (official-evidence panel is a different owner/lane): PVD's and VNM's own `financial_identity_citations.jsonl` entries disagree with their retained provider values despite p3f13 marking both `OFFICIAL_QUALIFIED` for the same identity — PVD's cited value (635,711,153) is implausibly small for total equity and may be a citation data-entry error. No new provider, no new official-evidence acquisition, no VALUE activation, no ticker-specific branch. **Corporate Intelligence catalyst/event/risk decision axis V1 (2026-09-05):** `CORPORATE_INTELLIGENCE_CATALYST_EVENT_RISK_DECISION_INTEGRATION_V1 = COMPLETE / PARTIAL_BY_EVIDENCE`, commit `8d62313` on top of `3d95749`, local-only/unpushed. New `current_corporate_intelligence_axis.py` normalizes the existing, previously-uncoordinated corporate-event stack (`current_corporate_event_context.py`, `market_wide_current_corporate_intelligence.py`, `bitemporal_semantic_contract.py`) into one canonical event taxonomy, a 7-value status ladder, deterministic catalyst/risk/mixed/informational classification, and materiality that fails closed to `POTENTIALLY_MATERIAL` (never `MATERIAL`, since no compatible amount-vs-denominator comparison exists in retained evidence). Wired additively into `integrated_investment_decision_product.py` as a 9th `CORPORATE_INTELLIGENCE` evidence axis, built with its own local try/except in `canonical_post_close_pipeline.py` so a corporate-evidence failure cannot cascade into the whole Integrated Decision build (proven live); `decide_research_action_posture`'s signature is unchanged (verified by direct inspection). No provider, PIT, liquidity, sizing, universal score, probability, or authority-promotion change; `research_action_posture` is unchanged. **Current evidence-bound scenario engine V1 (2026-08-24):** `CURRENT_EVIDENCE_BOUND_SCENARIO_ENGINE_V1 = COMPLETE_LOCALLY / RESEARCH_ONLY`. Every case is conditional, source-identity-bound, and has `probability_status=UNKNOWN_UNCALIBRATED`; Base is explicitly a current-continuation reference, never a most-likely claim. It preserves current tactical confirmation/invalidation, retained peer-relative and fundamental availability, valuation non-discrimination, and catalyst gaps verbatim through an opt-in Producer→Consumer pass-through. No target, expected return, ranking, recommendation, sizing, portfolio, execution, outcome, or calibration authority is created.

This review set remains distinct from full `PRIORITY_NOW`, entry-relevant records, strategy-lane queues, and final human selection. It keeps full-universe discovery distinct from the watchlist and passes per-ticker cards opt-in through Producer→Consumer. Entry actions remain existing non-actionable tactical states; probability remains `UNKNOWN_UNCALIBRATED`; no recommendation, target, ranking, sizing, portfolio, execution, or authority promotion is created.

**Daily Research Session Operations V1 (2026-08-24):** `DAILY_RESEARCH_SESSION_OPERATIONS_V1 = COMPLETE_LOCALLY / RETAINED_EVIDENCE_ONLY`. `tools/run_daily_research_session_operation.py` is the foreground entry point: it resolves an explicit identity-bound session registry, rejects mismatched/superseded upstream lineage, rebuilds peer/scenario/product surfaces coherently, validates Consumer pass-through, seals the existing immutable prospective snapshot, and writes an immutable run manifest. The prior 763/1510 product coverage was a superseded pre-recovery descriptive artifact; the accepted recovered 2026-08-21 artifact is bound by screening and tactical lineage and has 956 same-session technical records. Fundamental context is explicitly retained/undated and catalyst evidence is explicitly earlier/degraded; neither is silently treated as current-session market evidence.

**Market-wide Current Corporate Intelligence and Catalyst V1 (2026-08-24):** `MARKET_WIDE_CURRENT_CORPORATE_INTELLIGENCE_AND_CATALYST_V1 = COMPLETE_LOCALLY / RETAINED_EVIDENCE_PARTIAL`. The current 1,683-record deterministic artifact joins only retained, source-linked corporate-action chains: one current HPG executed corporate-action event, VNM historical completed dividend evidence, and VCB historical approved-but-not-completion-proven stock-dividend evidence. Facts, event lifecycle, and neutral catalyst descriptors remain separate; record date is never an ex-date, and approval/planning is never execution. Ownership and management/governance-change coverage remain explicitly unavailable rather than inferred. The exact artifact identity is selected by the daily-session registry, feeds a neutral `CATALYST_OR_EVENT` driver plus concise product-card context, passes through the Consumer fail-closed, and emits a separate corporate-intelligence prospective identity context without changing the frozen 2026-08-21 decision snapshot. No event feed, source authority, price/PIT, recommendation, probability, target, ranking, sizing, execution, or causal/outcome authority is created.

**Polymorphic Current Strategy Classification V1 (2026-08-24):** `POLYMORPHIC_CURRENT_STRATEGY_CLASSIFICATION_ENGINE_V1 = COMPLETE_LOCALLY / CURRENT_RESEARCH_ONLY`. A 1,683-record deterministic strategy surface reuses current technical/tactical states, retained fundamental trajectories, strict valuation availability, current evidence-bound scenarios, peer context, and Corporate Intelligence. It evaluates independent declared requirements for `TREND_MOMENTUM`, `BREAKOUT`, `EARLY_REVERSAL`, `BASE_ACCUMULATION`, `FUNDAMENTAL_IMPROVEMENT`, `EVENT_DRIVEN`, and `VALUE`; a ticker may retain multiple fits without a best-strategy choice or score. Strategy is explicitly distinct from tactical entry, scenario probability, and portfolio action. Value stays blocked without authoritative current valuation; Event Driven requires retained official event evidence; missing inputs constrain only their dependent strategy. The session operation materializes the strategy artifact before Product V2, validates Consumer pass-through, and emits an additive prospective identity context without changing earlier snapshots.

## 2. Program Priority & Foundation Status

It re-derives nothing: every cell cites `market_data_source_authority`, `provider_price_basis_registry`, `dnse_ohlc_price_basis_capability`, `current_valuation_input_authority`, `market_volume_value_semantic_contract`, `market_capability_taxonomy`, `dnse_fhsc_volume_basis`, `dnse_fhsc_market_composition_scaleout`, or `official_corporate_action_ledger`. The documented `G1`(round-lot)/`G4`(odd-lot)/`T1`,`T3`(put-through round-lot)/`T4`,`T6`(put-through odd-lot) board mapping is verified unchanged; numeric per-board composition remains unavailable (its only historical source commit is `SOURCE_GENERATOR_NOT_IN_CURRENT_MAIN_ANCESTRY`) and DNSE traded value remains wholly absent. `QUALIFIED_LIQUIDITY_INPUTS`, `POSITION_SIZING_IS_SAFE`, and `RAW_AS_TRADED` are unconditionally re-confirmed `False`/`False`/`NOT_PROMOTED` across all 11 rows by `assert_registry_fail_closed()`. **DNSE trades/liquidity basis qualification V1 (2026-08-23):** `dnse_trades_liquidity_basis.py` (`tools/derive_dnse_trades_liquidity_basis.py`) is a new, small, bounded live-probe adapter for the `trades_latest`/`trades_history` endpoints already registered in `dnse_market_data.MARKET_DATA_ENDPOINTS` (`family=trades`) -- it does not resurrect the orphaned `dnse_trades_canonical_shadow.py` (commit `2b7b38772e16c434c8adf5288cbc46ef0f7f4c02`, still `SOURCE_GENERATOR_NOT_IN_CURRENT_MAIN_ANCESTRY`), which is used only as implementation archaeology. A bounded 23-call live run (HPG, VCB, SSI, FPT, QNS; session 2026-08-21, plus HPG/VCB/SSI at the already-FHSC-cross-checked 2026-08-14) found: (1) `trades_latest` returns each board's own most-recent tick with already board-scoped *cumulative* session counters, so one non-paginated call per ticker recovers a complete current-session per-board volume/value reading; (2) `10 x G1.totalVolumeTraded` equals that session's DNSE daily OHLC `v` exactly across all 5 fresh, cross-sector tickers -- independent live corroboration of the existing shadow `C5` candidate (`dnse_volume_composition_reconciliation.C5_CANDIDATE`), which remains `EMPIRICAL_CANDIDATE` / `semantic_unit_interpretation=UNKNOWN` and is **not** promoted; (3) bounded `trades_history` pagination toward a *complete* session reconstruction is activity-dependent: a low-activity name (QNS) fully exhausts in 4 pages (387 rows spanning the whole 09:16-14:59 session, confirming a true zero put-through session -- zero-vs-missing genuinely distinguished), while HPG/VCB/SSI at both 2026-08-19 and 2026-08-14 remain `PARTIAL_BOUNDED_SCAN` after the same 4-page cap (a closing-auction burst can advance only ~0.08 real seconds across 300 DESC rows), so historical multi-session board decomposition stays infeasible within any small bounded call budget; (4) `grossTradeAmount` is arithmetically self-consistent (`avgPrice_kvnd x totalVolumeTraded_raw / 100_000`, verified 5/5) but its true-VND-value reading is board-dependent and unresolved -- consistent with true cumulative value in billion VND for G1 specifically under the same x10 hypothesis, but 10x too high under that same reading for a directly-reported board -- so it is retained only as a non-authoritative direct candidate, never promoted, and no derived `PRICE_TIMES_EXECUTED_SHARES` value is ever computed without an explicit external lot-multiplier (which nothing here supplies). Numeric board composition (`MATCHED_ROUND_LOT`/`MATCHED_ODD_LOT`/`PUT_THROUGH_ROUND_LOT`/`PUT_THROUGH_ODD_LOT`, reusing `market_price_volume_basis_authority.assert_lot_and_route_not_conflated()` unchanged) is therefore newly readable live for the **current session only**; `CURRENT_SESSION_LIQUIDITY_RESEARCH = ELIGIBLE` (research-descriptive only) while `HISTORICAL_LIQUIDITY_RESEARCH`, `ADV_VOLUME_RESEARCH`, `ADTV_RESEARCH`, `POSITION_SIZING`, `EXECUTION_CAPACITY`, and `PIT_BACKTEST` remain `BLOCKED`/`UNKNOWN`, enforced by `assert_fail_closed()`. `QUALIFIED_LIQUIDITY_INPUTS = NO` and `POSITION_SIZING_IS_SAFE = NO` are unconditionally re-confirmed, unchanged. Retained FHSC evidence (`operations-review/dnse-fhsc-volume-basis-qualification-v1-20260821/`) is cited read-only for the shared 2026-08-14 session (no new FHSC call; FHSC remains `CREDENTIAL_BLOCKED`). The existing technical contract is reused unchanged, provider-scoped and `SHADOW_ONLY`; recovered records add only raw-response lineage. **Market-wide current fundamental research scale-out V1 (2026-08-23):** `MARKET_WIDE_CURRENT_FUNDAMENTAL_RESEARCH_SCALEOUT_V1 = COMPLETE_LOCALLY / COHERENT_PARTIAL / OWNER_REVIEW_REQUIRED_NOT_AUTHORITATIVE`. The already-existing fundamental-evidence estate (`fundamental_research_readiness.py` / P3-B, `p3f13_official_financial_evidence_scaleout.py`, `p3f10_fundamental_evidence_scaleout.py`, `two_tier_fundamental_research.py` / P3-F15) is joined into one deterministic, sector-aware, coverage-explicit `market_wide_current_fundamental_research.py` artifact and wired end-to-end through `export_ai_bundle.py` into the Consumer (`ai-core-private`), reusing the existing opt-in explicit-path/content-hash-verified attach convention (`--include-market-wide-current-fundamental-research` / `--market-wide-current-fundamental-research-path`) unchanged from `market_wide_current_liquidity_research`/`market_wide_current_descriptive_research`. It computes no new evidence: `p3f13_official_financial_evidence_scaleout.py` gains two additive keys (`refreshed_panel_data`, `refreshed_fundamental_readiness`, mirroring `p3e_fundamental_coverage_closeout.py`'s own existing convention) so its already-computed current 13-issuer panel is exposed rather than discarded after only its hash was kept; every other existing key, value, and test in that engine is unchanged (its own 6-test suite still passes). Over the fixed 523-candidate P3-F10/P3-F13 cohort: 13 issuers are `OFFICIAL_QUALIFIED` (94 exact metrics, 22 proxies, 49 missing, 9 sector-inapplicable `NOT_APPLICABLE`, full per-metric lineage/periods/family-state), 507 are `PROVIDER_RESEARCH`, and 3 are `BLOCKED` (no retained source at all). Evidence presence and usable-metric presence are reported as two distinct numbers, never conflated: PNJ and FPT are both `OFFICIAL_QUALIFIED` yet both independently resolve `fundamental_research_readiness = BLOCKED` (every metric MISSING on a single retained period or an unreconciled input), which is why `issuers_with_official_facts = 13` but `issuers_with_usable_deterministic_metrics = 11`. Provider rows now add only the explicitly allowed `provider_series_growth` research: same-ticker, same-provider, same-canonical-metric, consecutive-quarter trends from the P3-F10-pinned retained canonical periods. 500 provider issuers have one or more of 1,236 available trend metrics (90 revenue growth, 78 earnings growth, 1,000 assets/equity directions, 68 operating-cash-flow directions); 1,299 are retained as explicit blocked trends (471 no reported series, 815 no same-provider consecutive pair, 13 non-positive growth base). Every trend holds ticker, provider, periods, method, `PROVIDER_RESEARCH`, status, fact/observation lineage, limitations, comparability scope, and exact blocked reason; no absolute provider value is labelled `OFFICIAL_QUALIFIED`, and no cross-provider, currency, scale, consolidated/separate, duration, or accounting-identity equivalence is assumed. Because P3-F10's frozen 2026-08-20 checkpoint predates PNJ/FPT's P3-F13 promotion, the join always prefers the current P3-F13 truth for any ticker it covers and records an explicit `supersedes_frozen_p3f10_disposition` note (also correcting PNJ's stale `sector="unknown"` tag to `corporate`) rather than serving a stale per-ticker view. Bank/securities sector-inapplicable metrics (e.g. VCB's `debt_to_equity`, `net_debt`, `cash_flow_to_earnings`) remain `NOT_APPLICABLE`, never missing or zero. No official evidence acquisition, source-route discovery, or provider-semantic promotion was reopened; `MARKET_WIDE_FINANCIAL_EVIDENCE_AND_SEMANTIC_COVERAGE_V1`'s 2026-08-22 `CLOSED_PARTIAL` gate is unchanged. No valuation, ranking, recommendation, probability, sizing, or backtest output exists (verified by an explicit absence test). Focused Producer and Consumer contract tests pass. **Watchlist tactical entry-state classifier V1 (2026-08-23):** `WATCHLIST_TACTICAL_ENTRY_DECISION_V1 = COMPLETE_LOCALLY / COHERENT_PARTIAL / OWNER_REVIEW_REQUIRED_NOT_AUTHORITATIVE`. Turns the already-computed current market-wide descriptive/screening/fundamental research into one deterministic nine-state tactical entry classifier, wired end-to-end through `export_ai_bundle.py` into the Consumer (`ai-core-private`), reusing the existing opt-in explicit-path/content-hash-verified attach convention unchanged from every sibling `market_wide_current_*` lane (`--include-watchlist-tactical-entry-classifier` / `--watchlist-tactical-entry-classifier-path`). New `watchlist_tactical_entry_classifier.py` computes no new technical indicator, ranking, or evidence: it reads only `market_wide_current_descriptive_research` (technical features, trend state, breadth/regime descriptors, descriptive liquidity), `current_market_screening_opportunity_comparison_foundation` (market-relative and sector-relative momentum/volume percentile context), and `market_wide_current_fundamental_research` (official/provider fundamental tier), joined and identity-verified against the two upstream lineage chains independently -- the fundamental cohort (523 P3-F10/P3-F13 candidates) is a structurally distinct universe from the descriptive/screening cohort (1,510 denominator), so a ticker's absence from it is reported as an explicit `NOT_IN_FUNDAMENTAL_COHORT` boundary, never a session/denominator mismatch.

Two output layers per ticker: `ticker_structure_state` (five raw trend/momentum-posture values, derived only from `trend_state` and `momentum_20d`'s sign plus a close-vs-MA20 proximity test reusing `price_structure_breakout_context.NEAR`'s existing 2% "near" convention rather than inventing a new threshold) and `entry_state` (the required nine-state taxonomy: `DOWNTREND`, `SELLING_PRESSURE_EASING`, `EARLY_REVERSAL_CANDIDATE`, `BASE_BUILDING`, `SIDEWAYS_NEUTRAL`, `BREAKOUT_READY`, `UPTREND_CONFIRMED`, `DISTRIBUTION_RISK`, `BREAKDOWN_RISK`), produced by a single ordered, gapless, first-match-wins decision table over already-computed signals only (exhaustively verified against all 1,080 combinatorial input states plus a real run over the full 1,683-candidate universe). `action` is a fixed nine-to-seven lookup from `entry_state` (`EARLY_ENTRY`, `BUY_ON_CONFIRMATION`, `ACCUMULATE_IN_BASE`, `HOLD_DO_NOT_ADD`, `WAIT`, `AVOID`, `REDUCE_EXIT`) -- `EARLY_ENTRY` is reserved for `EARLY_REVERSAL_CANDIDATE` per this milestone's own instruction that a confirmed uptrend must never be a prerequisite for it, and `is_full_position_ready` is structurally `False` for every `entry_state` except `BREAKOUT_READY`, and even then only with eligible liquidity, `OFFICIAL_QUALIFIED` fundamental readiness, and a non-risk-off `market_state` -- so `EARLY_ENTRY`/`ACCUMULATE_IN_BASE` can never carry a full-position claim, enforced in code (both Producer's gate function and Consumer's independent revalidation) rather than only asserted in prose. `market_state` (breadth/regime context reused from `market_wide_current_descriptive_research.market_breadth`'s own descriptors, shared by every ticker in one build) is descriptive context only and never gates or overrides a ticker's own `entry_state`. Missing/`BLOCKED` fundamental-tier context narrows `horizon` toward the shortest tier and lowers `data_quality.confidence`; it never by itself forces `WAIT` when tactical evidence is otherwise sufficient. `evidence_for`/`evidence_against`/`confirmation_trigger`/`invalidation` are deterministic string templates citing only already-computed values -- no free-form generation, no fabricated probability, target price, or portfolio-sizing formula anywhere (verified by an explicit key-absence test over the full real 1,683-record artifact). `EARLY_REVERSAL_CANDIDATE`/`BASE_BUILDING` carry early/base language and a stricter, faster `invalidation` than a confirmed-trend state; no state is ever described as a confirmed bottom or top.

Real run over the actual retained 2026-08-21-session artifacts (`market_wide_current_descriptive_research` rebuilt checkpoint, `current_market_screening_opportunity_comparison_foundation`, `market_wide_current_fundamental_research`): 1,683 candidates, 956 classified (exactly matching the descriptive lane's own same-session technical coverage count), 727 insufficient-data; `entry_state` distribution `BASE_BUILDING=77, BREAKDOWN_RISK=44, BREAKOUT_READY=40, DISTRIBUTION_RISK=66, DOWNTREND=109, EARLY_REVERSAL_CANDIDATE=30, SELLING_PRESSURE_EASING=139, SIDEWAYS_NEUTRAL=274, UPTREND_CONFIRMED=177`; `market_state=MIXED_NO_CLEAR_MARKET_REGIME` (breadth mixed, momentum breadth negative); `full_position_ready_count=0` -- an honest, evidence-driven zero, not a defect: only 13 of the 523 fundamental-cohort tickers are `OFFICIAL_QUALIFIED` today and none of today's 40 `BREAKOUT_READY` tickers happen to be among them. Spot-checked real tickers read coherently (e.g. VNM: `UPTREND_CONFIRMED`/`HOLD_DO_NOT_ADD`, momentum 0.1579 in the market and sector `UPPER_QUARTILE`; HPG/VCB/QNS: `SIDEWAYS_NEUTRAL`/`WAIT`, price within 2% of the 20-day moving average).

New `tools/run_watchlist_tactical_entry_classifier.py` runner (offline, deterministic, foreground). Consumer (`ai-core-private`): `builders/build_ticker_context.py` gains `watchlist_tactical_entry_classifier_contract`/`apply_bundle_*`, revalidating (never merely trusting) that `EARLY_ENTRY`/`ACCUMULATE_IN_BASE` never carry `is_full_position_ready=true`; `prompts/ai_analysis_templates.md` gains a new lane paragraph plus six prohibited-claims items (no confirmed-bottom/top language, no full-position claim under `EARLY_ENTRY`/`ACCUMULATE_IN_BASE`, no fabricated probability/target price/sizing, `market_state` never a forecast or an `entry_state` override, missing fundamental evidence never proof a setup is invalid, never an automated trade/execution instruction). 38 new Producer tests (`tests/test_watchlist_tactical_entry_classifier.py`, `tests/test_export_ai_bundle_watchlist_tactical_entry_classifier.py`) and 31 new Consumer tests (`tests/test_watchlist_tactical_entry_classifier_contract_pass_through.py`, `tests/test_watchlist_tactical_entry_classifier_frozen_time_e2e.py` -- a real cross-repo E2E over the actual 11-ticker production cohort, byte-identical Producer-to-Consumer, plus a real full-1,683-record absence-of-fabricated-output proof --, `tests/test_watchlist_tactical_entry_classifier_ai_prompt_contract.py`) all pass. A targeted Producer sweep (this file plus every sibling `market_wide_current_*`/screening/export_ai_bundle test, 244 tests) reproduces the exact same 6 pre-existing failures confirmed byte-identical via `git stash` against the unmodified pre-milestone checkout (stale VCB/VNM/HPG evidence-state and share-basis fixtures); a full 730-test Consumer sweep reproduces the same 10 pre-existing failures, likewise confirmed via `git stash` (unrelated metadata-registry-shadow/phase7-8-9-11 batch/catalog fixtures) -- neither is a regression introduced here. `py_compile` clean in both repos. No official evidence acquisition, source-route discovery, provider-semantic promotion, valuation, ranking, ordinal recommendation, probability, target price, portfolio-sizing formula, RAW_AS_TRADED, PIT, or backtest authority is created anywhere in this milestone. No push, merge, deploy, or production write in either repository. Artifact: `operations-review/watchlist-tactical-entry-decision-v1-20260823/watchlist_tactical_entry_classifier_artifact.json` (`watchlist_tactical_entry_classifier:4797deeb1f8b0bddd0d468ac98790dabf46e7f68f973ec4c0ad2549250d8a099`).

**Watchlist tactical decision closeout V1 (2026-08-23, same day):** `WATCHLIST_TACTICAL_DECISION_CLOSEOUT_V1 = COMPLETE_LOCALLY / COHERENT_PARTIAL`. Four corrections to the classifier above, made before its first use against the actual configured watchlist for the 2026-08-24 open; full rationale in `docs/DECISIONS.md`'s same-dated "Watchlist Tactical Decision Closeout" entry. (1) New `entry_action` (fixed 9→5 lookup, `ENTRY_ACTION_BY_ENTRY_STATE`) is now the PRIMARY "should I enter" field, never `HOLD_DO_NOT_ADD`/`REDUCE_EXIT`; the original `action` (unchanged values) is now documented as SECONDARY and position-management-conditional everywhere it appears. (2) `is_full_position_ready` is now unconditionally `False` and new `position_sizing_status` unconditionally `"NOT_EVALUATED"` for every record (position sizing remains not implemented), replacing the old BREAKOUT_READY-conditional gate; enforced independently in both Producer and Consumer. (3) Audited and confirmed unchanged: fundamental tier never gates `entry_state`/`entry_action`, only narrows `horizon`/`data_quality.confidence`. (4) `EARLY_REVERSAL_CANDIDATE` (`R6`) now requires an independent confirming signal (market-relative momentum, sector-relative momentum via a new `sector_momentum_bucket` rule input, or today's return+volume) beyond the bare momentum-sign flip, falling to new rule `R6B`/`SIDEWAYS_NEUTRAL` when unconfirmed; `BASE_BUILDING` (`R7`) now additionally requires no bottom-quartile relative momentum and no confirmed-down session today. `EARLY_ENTRY` remains reachable without a confirmed uptrend, unchanged.

Real before/after on the full retained 2026-08-21 session (1,683 candidates, 956 classified, regenerated offline from the same three retained source artifacts, zero network calls): `entry_state` distribution `BASE_BUILDING=20` (was 77), `BREAKDOWN_RISK=44`, `BREAKOUT_READY=40`, `DISTRIBUTION_RISK=66`, `DOWNTREND=140` (was 109), `EARLY_REVERSAL_CANDIDATE=30` (unchanged), `SELLING_PRESSURE_EASING=165` (was 139), `SIDEWAYS_NEUTRAL=274` (unchanged), `UPTREND_CONFIRMED=177` (unchanged) -- the 57 tickers leaving `BASE_BUILDING` land exactly in `DOWNTREND` (+31) and `SELLING_PRESSURE_EASING` (+26), confirmed by exact arithmetic; `R6B` is real and tested but does not fire on this session's actual data (every real `EARLY_REVERSAL_CANDIDATE` already carried a confirming signal). `entry_action` distribution: `EARLY_ENTRY=30`, `ACCUMULATE_IN_BASE=20`, `BUY_ON_CONFIRMATION=40`, `WAIT=1409`, `AVOID=184`. `full_position_ready_count=0` (now unconditional). New artifact identity: `watchlist_tactical_entry_classifier:bcc1e855f069e13b80d3e4b5b9c523a489356426fc32891a12c592265e7bc885` (regenerated in place over the prior `4797deeb...` checkpoint, same path).

Frozen pre-open artifact for the actual configured 11-ticker production cohort (`export_ai_bundle.py`'s `DEFAULT_TICKERS`: POW, SSI, HPG, EVF, PAN, PNJ, FPT, QNS, VNM, PVD, NVL; all 11 present/classified): 1 `ACCUMULATE_IN_BASE` (PAN), 10 `WAIT` (POW/HPG/EVF/QNS/PVD `SIDEWAYS_NEUTRAL`; SSI/PNJ/FPT `DISTRIBUTION_RISK`; VNM/NVL `UPTREND_CONFIRMED`), 0 `EARLY_ENTRY`/`BUY_ON_CONFIRMATION`/`AVOID`. Artifact (local-only, gitignored): `operations-review/watchlist-tactical-entry-decision-preopen-20260824/watchlist_tactical_entry_decision_preopen_20260824.json`.

42 Producer tests (was 38; +4, two of which are a new independent small cohort proving R6/R7 are genuinely tighter, not merely redocumented) and 38 Consumer tests (was 31; +7) all pass, including the real cross-repo frozen-time E2E over the regenerated artifact and the real 11-ticker cohort. A targeted 356-test Producer sweep and a full 736-test Consumer sweep reproduce the identical 6/10 pre-existing failures by name (same families as the classifier milestone above) -- neither is a regression. `py_compile` clean in both repos. No new technical indicator (`sector_momentum_bucket` is an already-computed value newly wired into the rule function), ranking, score, probability, target price, valuation, sizing formula, RAW_AS_TRADED/PIT, backtest, or new data acquisition anywhere. No push, merge, deploy, or production write in either repository; local checkpoint commits only.

## 3. Active Blockers & Invariant Governance Rules

1. **Price Basis Invariant**: `RAW_AS_TRADED` is **NOT PROMOTED**. Bounded REST OHLC remains `ADJUSTED_RETROSPECTIVE`. Unpromoted price fields fail closed for point-in-time backtesting.
2. **Liquidity & Turnover Invariant**: `QUALIFIED_LIQUIDITY_INPUTS = NO` and `POSITION_SIZING_IS_SAFE = NO`. Volume data is restricted to display and within-series analytics (`legacy.rel_vol`); it must never drive execution sizing or market liquidity metrics.
3. **Active Universe Invariant**: `ACTIVE_UNIVERSE` remains `UNKNOWN` for all instruments because DNSE feeds do not carry official exchange or listing-status proof.
4. **Temporal Freshness Invariant**: Freshness is determined by domain rules and market session calendars (`freshness_history.py`); naive `date < today => stale` is strictly prohibited.
5. **No Speculative Inference**: Ex-dates must never be inferred from record dates; debt fields must never be invented; missing independent measurements cannot be turned into evidence.
6. **Price Representation vs Price Basis Invariant** (2026-08-21): K-VND→VND unit representation
   (`price_representation_contract.py`, `contract_basis_tier = owner_directed_contractual_assumption`)
   is independent of adjustment/RAW_AS_TRADED/PIT price basis (Invariant 1,
   `provider_price_basis_registry.py`). A capability may be `RESEARCH_USABLE` for its canonical VND
   representation while remaining `NOT_QUALIFIED`/`NOT_PROMOTED` for RAW_AS_TRADED or PIT; converting
   a unit never promotes either, and neither may be inferred from the other. The K-VND→VND factor is
   always resolved by an explicit `(source, capability, instrument_class)` contract lookup, never by
   the numeric magnitude of the value being converted.

### 3.1 Global Readiness Rebaseline

- **`MINIMUM_VIABLE_ANALYSIS_SHADOW`** is available only for deterministic, current descriptive research with retained lineage. Every MVA artifact must carry: `is_actionable_for_execution=false`, `pit_backtest_eligible=false`, `liquidity_sizing_authority=BLOCKED`, and `valuation_scope=CURRENT_DESCRIPTIVE_ONLY`. It may operate while historical PIT, liquidity, and sizing remain blocked.
- **`FULL_DECISION_SUPPORT_READY`** requires all of the following, not merely an MVA artifact: current-share authority, canonical-universe authority, qualified volume/traded-value composition, historical RAW_AS_TRADED/PIT price and corporate-action authority, historical universe/entity PIT, liquidity/risk/sizing authority, and validated PIT backtesting.
- Any empirical active cohort is a **derived shadow denominator**, never `ACTIVE_UNIVERSE` authority. It must declare as-of date, lookback/window, source completeness, inclusion rule, and deterministic identity; no fixed count is governance truth.
- Current DNSE prices support bounded `CURRENT_DESCRIPTIVE_ONLY` use. Historical `RAW_AS_TRADED`/PIT remains unpromoted, and volume/traded-value composition remains insufficient for liquidity or sizing.
- `interbank_on_rate`, `sbv_net_injection_20d`, and `vn30f1m_basis` are source-qualification backlog fields only. No source authority or acquisition path is activated here.

### Evidence-Gated Research Decision Workflow V1 (2026-08-22)

`EVIDENCE_GATED_RESEARCH_DECISION_WORKFLOW_V1 = READY_FOR_REVIEW` locally.  The reusable
`evidence_gated_research_decision_workflow.py` contract integrates the retained, dated
2026-08-20 523-member `EMPIRICAL_ACTIVE_SHADOW_ONLY` research cohort into one deterministic
per-instrument packet.  It carries the cohort identity/source/membership state, retained evidence
lineage, independent eligibility dimensions, existing strategy-research lanes, orthogonal setup
context, scenario state, structured thesis/counter-thesis evidence, valuation boundary, blockers,
and a mandatory human-review gate.

- It reuses (and does not recalculate) the daily research, eligibility, setup, scenario, event,
  downside, market-context, and exact-session MVA artifacts.  The separate 2026-08-21 524-member
  shadow snapshot is explicitly not substituted for, or combined with, this dated cohort.
- Coverage is 523 current descriptive and market-context packets; the sector-neutral P3-F13
  official financial-evidence panel is 13 `ELIGIBLE` / 510 `BLOCKED` (not 11/512).  Fundamental
  model readiness is independently 13 `PARTIAL` / 510 `BLOCKED`; its P3-B metric-level matrix
  retains three `NOT_APPLICABLE` corporate-model metrics for VCB and three for SSI; the MVA proxy
  separately retains three sector-inapplicable industrial valuation methods for each.  Thus evidence
  presence, model applicability, `UNKNOWN`, and `BLOCKED` are never conflated.  Scenario remains
  25 partial evidence-bound packets and event evidence 1 retained packet; liquidity/PIT remain
  blocked for all 523.  These are independent gates, so no blocked gate hides unrelated current
  descriptive research.
- Valuation remains `NON_AUTHORITATIVE_RESEARCH_PROXY` only, with the explicit
  `PROVIDER_REPORTED_ISSUED_SHARES_PROXY` namespace; authoritative current-common share and market
  cap authority remain blocked.  Bank/securities industrial EV/revenue methods retain
  `NOT_APPLICABLE`, rather than being represented as missing or zero.
- Every packet is `RESEARCH_PARTIAL` and requires a human decision.  It emits no BUY/SELL/HOLD,
  target, expected return, scenario probability, position size, execution, liquidity, or PIT
  authority.  No new evidence, source route, data acquisition, or upstream authority is created.

### Evidence-Bound AI Research & Human Review V1 (2026-08-22)

`EVIDENCE_BOUND_AI_RESEARCH_AND_HUMAN_REVIEW_V1 = READY_FOR_REVIEW` locally.  The downstream
`evidence_bound_ai_research_human_review.py` contract consumes only the content-addressed
evidence-gated decision workflow packet, creates a whitelisted AI-input packet, validates an
untrusted structured draft deterministically, and requires a human review record before any
internal research disposition.

- The 2026-08-20 empirical-active 523-member cohort yields 523 deterministic AI inputs; the
  distinct 2026-08-21 524-member shadow snapshot is not mixed in.  No live model call is required
  or made.  No draft prose is synthesized across the cohort: 523 inputs are explicitly
  `MODEL_DRAFT_PENDING`, not evidence-insufficient.

  counter-evidence, changed eligibility states, proxy-as-authoritative valuation, forbidden
  recommendation/target/probability/sizing/execution output, and blocked liquidity/PIT claims.
- Human review states are bounded to `DRAFT`, `HUMAN_REVIEW_REQUIRED`,
  `NEEDS_MORE_EVIDENCE`, `APPROVED_FOR_INTERNAL_RESEARCH`, and `REJECTED`.  Human edits append
  `HUMAN_EDIT` provenance; they do not overwrite the machine draft.  Approval is internal
  research workflow only, never investment, portfolio, or execution authority.

### Prospective Research Case & Learning Ledger V1 (2026-08-22)

`PROSPECTIVE_RESEARCH_CASE_AND_LEARNING_LEDGER_V1 = READY_FOR_REVIEW` locally.  The new
`prospective_research_case_learning_ledger.py` contract begins prospective case history from the

### Current Portfolio Risk Envelope V1

The optional daily-session portfolio branch is complete locally. It consumes only explicit holdings/exposure, emits deterministic concentration and retained-evidence context, and remains descriptive/non-actionable. Liquidity, correlation, portfolio volatility, VaR/CVaR, sizing, leverage, and execution remain blocked or not evaluated. The retained fixture is explicitly `DEMONSTRATION_NOT_REAL_HOLDINGS`, not a live portfolio or learning input.

### Current Macro Regime and Cross-Asset Context V1

`current_macro_regime/v1` is a source-bound current-research macro artifact. `vietnam_macro_observation/v1` now retains two dated National Statistics Office CPI releases (June and July 2026) and its release-calendar payload; July CPI year-on-year (4.45%, published 2026-08-03) is current descriptive context through the retained 2026-09-03 next-release date, with date-level—not intraday—PIT qualification after publication. The CPI axis may use its retained June predecessor (4.69%) only. SBV policy-rate, central-FX, credit-growth, system-liquidity, and official bond-yield metric contracts remain unavailable; they are never proxied. A macro artifact released or retrieved after an equity session is passed into that session only as explicitly unavailable context. No macro forecast, causal attribution, macro beta, sizing, or execution authority is created.

### Current Market Flow / Positioning V1

`current_market_flow_positioning/v1` is a deterministic, provider-scoped current-research projection over retained canonical DNSE/FHSC observations. It independently retains traded-value composition, DNSE foreign value flow, FHSC foreign-room, proprietary, and active-order context, with exact session/retrieval/raw identities and fail-closed missing/rejected/rate-limited/semantic-blocked states. The retained real 2026-08-21 three-name acquisition materialized all five dimensions for HPG/SSI/VCB; it is a bounded retained cohort, not a whole-market claim. Flow is optional evidence in scenario, strategy, explicit-portfolio context, Product V2, Consumer, and session manifests. No institutional intent, causality, liquidity, sizing, execution, PIT, backtest, or RAW_AS_TRADED authority is created.

**Scaleout closeout (2026-08-24):** `CURRENT_MARKET_FLOW_POSITIONING_SCALEOUT_V1 = COHERENT_PARTIAL_PROVIDER_LIMITED`. A two-request live session attestation proved the FHSC trading-history and DNSE foreign-trading response rows bind to the retained 2026-08-21 completed session; DNSE timestamp binding is now preserved through canonical provenance. A deterministic composite of all same-session retained waves creates 1,683 candidate records: 111 traded-value-ready, 14 active-order/proprietary-ready, 13 foreign-room-ready, and one session-attested DNSE foreign-flow-ready. Exact flow artifact identity is bound in the normal daily registry and the regenerated daily operation. The provider-supported cohort is never described as the whole market.

### Historical Matched Traded Value and ADV Authority V1 (2026-08-24)

`HISTORICAL_MATCHED_TRADED_VALUE_AND_ADV_AUTHORITY_V1 = COMPLETE_LOCAL / OUTCOME_C_NARROW_MATCHED_VALUE_UNLOCK / ADV20_NOT_READY`.

- The final retained DNSE Trades corpus is explicitly bounded to 40 sessions (2026-06-17 through 2026-08-11), 1,660 requested trade instruments, 66,373 complete logical ticker-session responses, and 27 retained provider failures.  A derived result may use a ticker-session only after its retained page cursor chain is contiguous and terminal; no session phase is inferred from endpoint defaults or time-of-day heuristics.
- The retained 2026-08-07/10/11 DNSE/FHSC overlap yields twelve exact observations across FPT, HPG, SSI, and VCB.  For each, `sum(G1.matchPrice * G1.matchQtty) * 10 * 1,000 VND` exactly equals both the independently retained FHSC matched volume (after the explicit G1 ×10 quantity transform) and the FHSC matched value.  This is a narrow empirical G1 matched-value observation contract, not a generic provider-wide value field.
- G4/T1/T3/T4/T6 rows are retained as actual board composition and are neither zero-filled nor folded into matched value.  Their distinct semantics and any put-through relationship remain outside this contract.  The 1,683-candidate ledger has 4 qualified tickers / 12 qualified ticker-sessions, 1,631 in-corpus names semantic-blocked outside the four-ticker FHSC-value anchor scope, and 48 current-universe names absent from the retained Trades universe.  The two retained universes are not silently treated as the same 1,660-name set.
- ADV20 emits no average: every qualified ticker has only 3 of 20 expected complete qualified sessions.  Liquidity, turnover, portfolio-risk dependency, execution, position sizing, PIT/backtest, RAW_AS_TRADED, valuation, and recommendation authority remain blocked or out of scope.  The next real data gate is 20 expected complete sessions under the same exact G1/FHSC matched-value reconciliation contract.
